AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='All changes are in place and the project builds.\n\n## Diagnosis\n\n`ItemTouchHelper` translates the swiped view off screen (`translationX = ±recyclerView.getWidth()`), and the fragments additionally dim it (`onItemSelected()` → `alpha = 0.5f`). That state is only undone in `ItemTouchHelper.onChildViewDetachedFromWindow()`, which calls `Callback.clearView()` — i.e. **only when the RecyclerView actually recycles/detaches the view during a layout pass**.\n\nWhen the swiped item was the *last* one, `QueueFragment.onFragmentLoaded()` did:\n\n```java\nif (queue == null || queue.size() == 0) {\n    recyclerView.setVisibility(View.GONE);   // <-- the defect\n    txtvEmpty.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);\n}\n```\n\nA view with visibility `GONE` is never measured or laid out by its parent, so the RecyclerView never ran the layout pass that would have detached the swiped child. `clearView()` was therefore never invoked and the view holder stayed attached, still translated fully off screen. Pressing **Undo** re-inserted the item, made the list visible again, and RecyclerView reused that very holder — exactly the reported symptom: *"Empty view is removed and list is displayed. List contains an item (layout inspector), but it seems to be moved outside of the screen."*\n\n## Fix\n\n**1. `QueueFragment.onFragmentLoaded()`** — stop hiding the list; only toggle the empty view, which already overlays the RecyclerView in `queue_fragment.xml` (declared after it, `match_parent`, transparent background). The RecyclerView now lays out when it becomes empty, recycles the swiped child, and `clearView()` resets the swipe state properly:\n\n```java\ntxtvEmpty.setVisibility(queue == null || queue.size() == 0 ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);\nrecyclerView.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);\n```\n\n**2. `QueueRecyclerAdapter` + `AllEpisodesRecycleAdapter`** — added a `resetSwipeState()` to each view holder (clears `alpha`, `translationX`, `translationY`) and call it at the top of `onBindViewHolder()`. This makes any (re-)bound row unconditionally visible, so a holder that retained a swipe offset can never render a restored item off screen. This covers the other affected screens, **Episodes→New** and **Episodes→Favorites**, which share `AllEpisodesRecycleAdapter`.\n\nThis is safe for in-progress gestures: `ItemTouchHelper` re-applies the translation of the selected view (and of running recover animations) on every draw frame via `Callback.onDraw()`, and drag reordering uses `notifyItemMoved()`, which repositions rather than rebinds.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 1569, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 65278, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 1569, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfAFh2ddjFhajwyQZdzex', stop_reason=None, session_id='8c50f587-81e8-4603-b364-f616b64582d9', uuid='dc64a5ee-b5cc-41eb-bcf5-782aaa773acf')
